# Logic Fit V2 — Implementation Status Report

## Manual UI handoff snapshot — 2026-10-15

This snapshot records the latest post-build, read-only visual handoff check. It does **not** replace the detailed screen matrix/history below, and it does not claim authenticated functional acceptance.

- **Build:** PASS with Node `v24.20.0` (`npm run build`).
- **Targeted component/ownership/i18n tests:** **64/64 PASS** (`branding-v2-default-theme`, `design-system-ownership`, `css-selector-ownership`, `i18n-runtime`, `login-css-ownership`, `register-v2-design-contract`).
- **Browser visual smoke:** PASS, generated by `scripts/qa-complete-ui.js` against the static, data-free QA server at `http://127.0.0.1:4173`. It inspected 19 Gym application roots, 9 Platform Admin panels, 6 registration steps, 6 member-portal surfaces, 9 Store views, 5 portal tools and 32 dialogs. It recorded **861 evidence entries** across widths 320, 360, 390, 430, 600, 768, 820, 1024, 1280, 1366, 1440, 1536 and 1920px. Light presentation was checked across the viewport matrix; Dark evidence was sampled at 390, 820 and 1440px. **0** detected page errors, console errors, failed same-origin responses or unexpected authorization responses; **0** layout assertions failed. The runner uses intercepted synthetic browser fixtures and is not an authenticated application test.
- **Accessibility follow-up:** Review of the capped DOM samples traced the repeated 415 observations to one placeholder-only search field in the Branding preview. It now receives an accessible name from the existing centralized Arabic/English translation key. A fresh post-fix audit recorded **0 unnamed interactive observations** across 861 evidence entries. This targeted name check is **not** a complete WCAG audit.
- **Fresh artifacts:** `qa/reports/logicfit-v2-ui-handoff-20261015-final/complete-ui-qa.json` and `qa/artifacts/logicfit-v2-ui-handoff-20261015-final/` (861 audit entries and representative screenshots). Earlier screenshots remain in `qa/artifacts/logicfit-v2-ui-handoff-20261015/`.
- **Print samples visually inspected:** Arabic RTL membership document and Store receipt, both rendered to actual A4 PDFs/previews, plus previously captured member receipt, coaching workout and portal report outputs. The coaching workout PDF’s second page contains only a reference card and footer; this is documented as a sparse-page presentation issue, not misreported as a clean pagination pass. No evidence found in this pass establishes A5 or thermal-paper output; do not claim those sizes are supported or verified. Print coverage remains partial for the full inventory, multilingual/long-data combinations and all template variants.
- **Legacy styling:** retired `visual-redesign.css`, `ui-foundation.css` and `design-foundation.css` have no active source entrypoint imports. Removed one proven duplicate `.phone-country-menu[hidden]` declaration; the canonical `[hidden]` owner remains in `reset.css`. The style validator still warns about `!important` use in 9 owner files (82 declarations remain after that removal); they were not bulk-removed. `theme.css` remains actively loaded and needs consumer-level review. Legacy CSS cleanup is not reported as complete.
- **Functional acceptance:** intentionally left to the user’s manual review, as requested. Existing matrix status remains **0/32 Functional QA Passed, 8/32 Blocked, 24/32 Not Tested, 0/32 Accepted**; visual smoke fixtures are not evidence of live authentication, permissions or business-flow outcomes.
- **Targeted browser regression after the `hidden` cleanup:** `phone-system-v2.spec.js --project=mobile --workers=1 --retries=0`: **8 PASS / 4 SKIP**, 0 failures. The skipped cases were reported as skips, not counted as passes.
- **Local Preview:** Express is available at `http://127.0.0.1:3010` with `NODE_ENV=test`, no cache, no schema/bootstrap startup and email disabled; the connection points to the local `LogicFit_QA_20260923` database. The health endpoint returned HTTP 200. Authenticated access is **BLOCKED**: the configured QA Owner login returned `401 INVALID_CREDENTIALS`; the existing credential helper refused to reset because it found 2 ambiguous active Owner candidates. No account or business record was changed after that fail-closed result. Do not use another candidate without an explicit identity choice. The static visual-only server at `http://127.0.0.1:4173/login` is running and returned HTTP 200; it uses mock public responses and cannot test authenticated or mutating workflows. No Preview/Staging deployment workflow was found. **No Production deployment was made.**
- **Git:** the reviewed V2 changes are committed on `codex/logic-fit-v2-ui-handoff-20261015`; implementation commit `30320fb835ab97b864f07226a73218fa16d43584` was pushed to that feature branch. No changes were pushed to `main`, and no Production deployment was triggered. The commit includes UI, design tokens, central presentation/i18n, V2 tests, and documentation; it excludes local QA reports/artifacts, temporary files, and the unrelated reference audit documents.

There are no `Complete`, `Passed`, `Accepted`, or `Not Started` rows in this snapshot. “32 with some V2 visual implementation” means each row has at least a scoped owner/token/layout change recorded; it does **not** mean its complete screen has been migrated. The exact row-level remaining work is in the final matrix column. Counts are not inferred from historical suite totals.

## Architecture and contracts preserved

- No API, endpoint, database schema, SQL business rule, permission, role, route contract, or server behavior was intentionally changed.
- The latest recorded full browser suite: **526 PASS / 74 SKIP / 0 FAIL**. The JSON reporter identifies **20 credential-gated `ui-ux.spec.js` cases** skipped because QA owner credentials are absent, plus **54 intentional project-specific skips** (e.g. desktop-only/mobile-only checks or tests whose matrix runs internally). No tests were removed or disabled for the run. This full E2E result predates the latest narrow Members localization/spacing change; the relevant current Members browser matrix passed separately.
- No database connection was available in this local environment (`DB_HOST`/`SQL_HOST`/`SQL_SERVER` were not configured), and the app could not be run against the local database.
- No production environment was used for testing or modified.

## Legacy design removal verification

**Global visual-layer removal: verified. Overall legacy migration: incomplete.** `public/css/components/visual-redesign.css`, `ui-foundation.css`, and `design-foundation.css` are absent; the active CSS source entrypoints do not import the retired global layers. The generated CSS bundles were rebuilt. The ownership suite asserts the source files/imports are absent and canonical selectors remain in their owners (**45/45 targeted unit assertions pass**). Earlier 153/165 textual consumer counts and notes that the visual layer was active are historical and superseded; textual selector matches were never proof of rendered use. This removal alone does not establish every former consumer's route-level visual acceptance. The broader feature-scoped legacy declaration/token-alias audit is still open; the 11 `!important` validator warnings require individual ownership/safety review and are not automatically deletable legacy CSS.

Other feature-specific CSS sources remain in use and still require route-by-route audit. The latest stylesheet validator checked 69 CSS sources and reported 11 existing `!important` declarations requiring review; they were retained where native-control, reduced-motion, QR, sticky-table, or plugin contracts require them. A full audit for duplicate tokens, legacy aliases, and visual drift remains open; no blanket deletion or zero-legacy claim is made. Generated assets were rebuilt by the established pipeline.

The comment-only `design-foundation.css` had no selectors or runtime consumers; its three build imports and ownership-test reference were removed, the file was deleted, and the consolidated stylesheet checkpoint was corrected. Build validated 70 CSS sources and all 635 unit tests pass. This is a small confirmed dead-layer cleanup, not complete legacy stylesheet cleanup or duplicate-component consolidation. Existing `!important` and duplicate-token warnings remain.

## Current master execution picture — 2026-10-13

This is a code-and-test-evidence snapshot of the current dirty local worktree, not a release claim. The 32 rows below follow the existing discovery inventory (13 entry surfaces + 19 Gym hash views). “Partial” means some V2 presentation or targeted QA exists; it does not mean screen acceptance. Visual “Passed” is intentionally unused where there is no complete per-screen visual sign-off across its required states. Functional “Passed” means a bounded test exists, not every live business action. `#` routes are rendered inside the Gym shell and have additional nested views listed below.

**Attendance metric wrapping — 2026-10-13:** Reproduced English summary labels extending beyond their metric cards at 320px (the “Today’s attendance” label exceeded its card by about 28px). The Attendance page owner now constrains and wraps metric labels using V2 typography tokens. The new real-browser read-only matrix passed **1/1 spec / 20 cells** (320/390/768/1024/1440 × Arabic/English × Light/Dark), with labels contained, no horizontal overflow, both attendance records preserved, no API writes and no browser errors. Lead inspected `qa/artifacts/attendance-v2-surface-visual/attendance-320-en-light.png`; matching measurements are in `matrix.json`. Other observed mobile shell/card composition is unchanged and remains outside this CSS fix. This is a bounded visual defect closure only; #14 remains Visual Partial / Functional Not Tested / Acceptance Partial.

**Coaching central i18n and stale asset correction — 2026-10-13:** The initial browser scenario continued to render stale Coaching strings because the feature manifest used a fixed `coaching.js?v=20` cache key. The canonical runtime asset-version builder now derives Coaching’s version from source content. Central translation hooks now cover the page heading, Add/Refresh, table headers and the external-trainee dialog title. Focused Playwright scenario **1/1 PASS**, 16 viewport/theme/locale captures, with search/dialog values and fixture data retained, zero locale-triggered feature reads/writes, and no page errors. The optional SweetAlert CDN request is intercepted and aborted by the local test; it is not a successful external request. Lead inspected `qa/artifacts/coaching-central-i18n/coaching-320-light-en.png`: dynamic record labels/actions remain Arabic and the compact layout still has overlap/spacing problems. This advances central i18n coverage to 24/32, but Coaching remains Visual Partial / Functional Not Tested / Acceptance Partial.

**Backup history visual QA — 2026-10-13:** `backup-history-v2-visual.spec.js` passed **1/1** locally across 16 viewport/theme/locale cells. The fixture blocked writes and external requests, refreshed with a read-only GET, confirmed no page-level horizontal overflow, and generated actual screenshots. Before/after 320px and 390px images were inspected; feature presentation uses a single-column responsive history card pattern while retaining the existing records, controls and status data. This is Visual QA Partial only: archive creation/restore/deletion and permission-specific outcomes were not exercised.

Lead-rerun `intelligence-v2-bounded-visual.spec.js` passed **1/1**, exercising 16 combinations of 320/390/768/1440px, Light/Dark and RTL/LTR, plus table-end horizontal reach; no writes or page errors. Only that bounded responsive defect is closed: #21 remains Visual Partial, Functional Not Tested and Acceptance Partial.

**Root entry visual root-cause fix — 2026-10-13:** `/` serves the shared auth document but its initial authenticated-shell stylesheet intentionally excludes the Login page owner; consequently the unauthenticated Login surface appeared unstyled at `/`. A first inline-import experiment added 80,448 bytes (+21.6%) to the shell bundle, so it was replaced before handoff with lazy loading of the existing generated `login-entry.css` only after the unauthenticated session decision. `build-runtime-asset-versions.js` now fingerprints that generated stylesheet in an HTML meta URL; the authenticated app-shell bundle returned to its prior **372,466 bytes**. Under Node `v24.20.0`, CSS build/style validation passed (69 sources, 11 existing `!important` review warnings), runtime-asset version units passed **5/5**, and read-only browser test `root-entry-v2-readonly.spec.js` passed **1/1** across 320/390/768/1440 × Light/Dark. Eight screenshots were captured under `qa/artifacts/root-entry-v2-readonly/`, the Gym/Trainer registration choices were confirmed, there was no horizontal overflow, and there were zero API writes. The 320px Light and 1440px Dark screenshots were visually inspected. This is fixture-driven layout QA, not proof of live authenticated data or complete individual-screen visual review. |

Evidence: `qa/artifacts/playwright/member-full-print-full-mem-16b1d-ts-its-real-template-to-PDF-desktop/member-full-v2-preview.pdf`, `final-print-page-1.png` through `final-print-page-4.png`, and the full preview screenshot. No missing/clipped fields observed; long activity history continues to page 4 with repeated table headers. |
| Diff whitespace | PASS — `git diff --check` (only existing line-ending notices). |

The prior `about:blank` print window could wait indefinitely on `document.fonts.ready`, and the external print stylesheet did not load in the isolated QA popup. `waitForPrintWindowFonts` now waits briefly for the stylesheet, fetches and inlines the same-origin source as a fallback (with a `<base>` so local font URLs resolve), then bounds font readiness so printing cannot remain blocked indefinitely. The real API/domain flow and printed data are unchanged.

The delivered CSS slices are `member-portal.css`, `trainer-workspace.css`, `platform-admin.css`, `store.css`, `reports.css`, `expenses.css`, `saas.css`, `library.css`, and `nutrition.css`; the print generator/fallback, full-member print test, and report also changed. The entire V2 migration is still **INCOMPLETE**: many screens and templates have not been individually migrated/visually accepted; legacy `visual-redesign.css` remains imported by both main bundles and still has consumers; token duplication/style warnings remain; there is no full-route print inventory verification, production candidate, or final release evidence.

No database, API contract, business logic, authentication, permissions, or RLS changed. No Git commit, push, or production deployment was made.

## Current status reconciliation — 2026-10-14 (latest evidence)

This addendum supersedes older dated totals above where they conflict. The **top-level visual migration is closed at 32/32**, while full operational acceptance remains **0/32 Accepted** because required authenticated workflows, permission outcomes and other business checks are not yet evidenced. Evidence counts below distinguish these gates.

### Current 32-screen totals

| Measure | Current count | Interpretation |
|---|---:|---|
| Screen rows inventoried | 32/32 | 13 entry surfaces + 19 Gym hash views; nested panels overlap and are not extra top-level screens. |
| Implementation Complete | 32/32 | Current row-level matrix marks the 32 top-level screens complete; nested targets and role-gated operations are tracked separately below. |
| Implementation Partial | 0/32 | No top-level row remains Partial for the scoped V2 visual implementation. |
| Visual QA Passed | 32/32 | Current row-level matrix cites actual screenshots/browser evidence for all top-level screens. This is visual acceptance, not authenticated business-workflow acceptance. |
| Visual QA Partial | 0/32 | No top-level screen remains Partial in the current matrix. Nested surfaces still have their own evidence gaps. |
| i18n Passed / Partial / Not Tested | **32 / 0 / 0** | The screen matrix records the central Arabic/English presentation checks as Passed; API/domain data remains unchanged and some deep ARIA/error/print permutations remain outside the per-screen matrix. |
| Responsive Passed / Partial / Not Tested | **32 / 0 / 0** | The screen matrix records responsive acceptance with row-specific viewport evidence; this does not claim every nested workflow/dialog permutation is covered. |

| Functional QA Blocked | 8/32 | Test/access prerequisites: approved isolated Owner/Assistant/Trainer or other role sessions are unavailable for the required checks. This is not evidence of a defect. |
| Functional QA Not Tested | 24/32 | Complete screen workflow has not been exercised with sufficient authorized, safe evidence. |
| Acceptance Accepted / Partial / Blocked | **0 / 32 / 0** | A screen stays Partial until all applicable visual and functional gates are met; blockers are recorded in the QA column and exact remaining-work cells. |

It does not close every business workflow, role-gated outcome, nested overlay, accessibility criterion or print variant. `/backup` has entry/history/dialog presentation evidence, while destructive restore/delete and full server lifecycle remain untested. ### Print pagination and Member Portal report follow-up — 2026-10-13
